MA	<p>4228_0bb3</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>SspI</i></span> is derived from Sphaerotilus species, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>RsaI</i></span> is derived from Rhodopseudomonas sphaeroides. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Strand Question:</strong></p><p>Examine the DNA strand shown below.
